# Wonderweb Studio — Complete Supplied SEO Inventory

Prepared: 6 November 2026. Companion to [the website and SEO plan](WONDERWEB_STUDIO_PLAN.md).

## How to use this inventory

- All 2,000 query records and 2,000 page records from the two search archives are retained in original export order. The query records contain 1,923 distinct values after trimming and lowercasing.
- Record IDs include property, table, and logical CSV record number, counting the header as record 1. For example, `BR-Q-0002` is the first data record in BuiltRight Queries.csv. Embedded newlines mean physical file line numbers can differ.
- Queries and pages are independent tables. No row pairing or query-to-ranking-page relationship is asserted.
- Topic, service, and intent labels are provisional keyword-pattern classifications for navigation. They have not received row-by-row editorial approval. Labels can be wrong, especially for names, ambiguous words, and multi-topic searches.
- No target URL is assigned automatically. Final target ownership and Create/Group/Resource/Brand/Variant/Defer/Exclude/Investigate decisions belong to the mapping stage in the main plan.
- Preserve each original property and period. Do not add query/page totals to the property total, combine periods as equivalent, or interpret impressions as monthly search volume.
- Exact text is retained except embedded whitespace is normalized to keep Markdown tables readable. Original CSVs remain authoritative for literal strings.
- Page URLs remain distinct, including query strings and trailing slash variants. These historical rows do not establish current URL status.
- These 1,000-row tables are capped exports, not a complete inventory of all searches or published pages. The full original archives remain in the project root.
